relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Connect to external tools with MCP" data-page-href="/en/agent-sdk/mcp" id="content"><span data-as="p">The <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/docs/getting-started/intro" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Model Context Protocol (MCP)</a> is an open standard for connecting AI agents to external tools and data sources. With MCP, your agent can query databases, integrate with APIs like Slack and GitHub, and connect to other services without writing custom tool implementations.</span>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers can run as local processes, connect over HTTP, or execute directly within your SDK application.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutNote"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">This page covers MCP configuration for the Agent SDK. To add MCP servers to the Claude Code CLI so they load in every project, see <a href="/docs/en/mcp#mcp-installation-scopes" class="link">MCP installation scopes</a>.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="quickstart" aria-labelledby="_R_79deehkd74t9_"><a href="#quickstart" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Quickstart"></a><span id="_R_79deehkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Quickstart</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">This example connects to the <a href="https://code.claude.com/docs" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Claude Code documentation</a> MCP server using <a href="#http%2Fsse-servers" class="link">HTTP transport</a> and uses <a href="#allow-mcp-tools" class="link"><code>allowedTools</code></a> with a wildcard to permit all tools from the server.</span>

<span data-as="p">The agent connects to the documentation server, searches for information about hooks, and returns the results.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="add-an-mcp-server" aria-labelledby="_R_f9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#add-an-mcp-server" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Add an MCP server"></a><span id="_R_f9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Add an MCP server</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">You can configure MCP servers in code when calling <code>query()</code>, or in a <code>.mcp.json</code> file loaded via <a href="#from-a-config-file" class="link"><code>settingSources</code></a>.</span>
<span id="in-code"></span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="add-a-server-in-code" aria-labelledby="_R_l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#add-a-server-in-code" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Add a server in code"></a><span id="_R_l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Add a server in code</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Pass MCP servers directly in the <code>mcpServers</code> option. This example starts a local filesystem MCP server for <code>/Users/me/projects</code>. Replace that path with a directory on your machine:</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-hidden="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" hidden="" id="base-ui-_R_kgp9deehkd74t9_" role="tabpanel" tabindex="0" inert="" data-index="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative focus-visible:outline-hidden before:content-[&#x27;&#x27;] before:absolute before:inset-0 before:z-10 before:pointer-events-none before:rounded-xt focus-visible:before:outline focus-visible:before:outline-1 focus-visible:before:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:before:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:before:outline-primary-light data-hidden:hidden [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:rounded-xt!"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:100%;background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-white"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_3kgp9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="0" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 focus-visible:outline-1! <span id="from-a-config-file"></span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="add-a-server-from-a-config-file" aria-labelledby="_R_t9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#add-a-server-from-a-config-file" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Add a server from a config file"></a><span id="_R_t9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Add a server from a config file</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Create a <code>.mcp.json</code> file at your project root. The file is picked up when the <code>project</code> setting source is enabled, which it is for default <code>query()</code> options. If you set <code>settingSources</code> explicitly, include <code>&quot;project&quot;</code> for this file to load. Replace <code>/Users/me/projects</code> with a directory on your machine:</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connection-timing" aria-labelledby="_R_139deehkd74t9_"><a href="#connection-timing" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Connection timing"></a><span id="_R_139deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Connection timing</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code starts connecting your MCP servers when the session starts, and sends the <a href="#error-handling" class="link">init message</a> once the first-turn wait, if any, ends. How long the first turn waits for a server depends on where you configured it. If you set <code>permissionPromptToolName</code>, the first turn always waits for that tool’s server, up to <code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code>, wherever you configured it.</span>
<span data-as="p">For a server in <code>options.mcpServers</code>, find its type in the first column to see how long the first turn waits for it.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_199deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">Server type</th><th style="text-align:left">Delays the first turn?</th><th style="text-align:left">First-turn wait timeout</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">stdio server, or HTTP/SSE server without a cached tool list</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes, until it connects</td><td style="text-align:left"><a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code></a>, 30 seconds by default; the connection fails at that deadline</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">Remote server with a cached tool list, saved by Claude Code from a previous connection</td><td style="text-align:left">No; the cached tools are available from the first turn</td><td style="text-align:left">None; connects on its first tool call, and that deferred connect has its own timeout</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">In-process <a href="#sdk-mcp-servers" class="link">SDK server</a></td><td style="text-align:left">Yes, until it connects and lists its tools</td><td style="text-align:left"><a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code></a>, 30 seconds by default, per connect attempt; the connection fails at that deadline</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">A server has a cached tool list only when the <a href="/docs/en/mcp#server-status-detail" class="link">MCP discovery cache</a> is on.</span>
<span data-as="p">Servers from <a href="#from-a-config-file" class="link">settings files</a> such as <code>.mcp.json</code>, or from plugins, often still show <code>pending</code> in the init message. How long the first turn waits for them depends on <code>options.mcpServers</code> and tool search:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>options.mcpServers</code> includes a stdio, HTTP, or SSE server</strong>: the first turn waits for the pending settings-file and plugin servers too, up to <code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Otherwise, when <code>ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL</code> points to a <a href="/docs/en/mcp#tool-search-through-a-gateway" class="link">gateway</a> and <a href="/docs/en/mcp#configure-tool-search" class="link"><code>ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH</code></a> is unset</strong>: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds for every pending settings-file and plugin server.</li>
<li><strong>Otherwise, with <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/tool-search" class="link">tool search</a> on, which is the default</strong>: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds, and only for pending servers that set <a href="/docs/en/mcp#exempt-a-server-from-deferral" class="link"><code>alwaysLoad: true</code></a> or that you <a href="#named-servers" class="link">named</a>. The rest keep connecting in the background, and Claude <a href="/docs/en/mcp#tool-availability" class="link">reaches their tools</a> once they connect.</li>
<li><strong>Otherwise, with tool search off</strong>: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds for every pending settings-file and plugin server. Tool search is off when your <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/tool-search#configure-tool-search" class="link"><code>ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH</code> value or model</a> turns it off, or when you exclude the <code>ToolSearch</code> tool, for example through <code>disallowedTools</code>.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="named-servers" aria-labelledby="_R_1h9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#named-servers" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Named servers"></a><span id="_R_1h9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Named servers</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">You name a server by listing any of its tools, such as <code>mcp__github__list_issues</code> or <code>mcp__github__*</code>, in <a href="#allow-mcp-tools" class="link"><code>allowedTools</code></a> or in a <code>permissions.allow</code> rule in the <code>settings</code> option. An <a href="/docs/en/hooks#mcp-tool-hook-fields" class="link"><code>mcp_tool</code> hook</a> also names the server it calls when you configure it on a prompt-submission, tool-use, permission, subagent, task, elicitation, or worktree event. Before v2.1.284, naming a server had no effect on the first-turn wait.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="set-the-first-turn-wait-yourself" aria-labelledby="_R_1l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#set-the-first-turn-wait-yourself" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Set the first-turn wait yourself"></a><span id="_R_1l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Set the first-turn wait yourself</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To choose the first-turn wait yourself, set <code>CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS</code> in the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/configuration#set-environment-variables" class="link"><code>env</code> option</a>, for example <code>CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS: &quot;5000&quot;</code>. The first turn then waits up to that many milliseconds for pending settings-file and plugin servers, whether or not tool search is on. The same limit replaces the <code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code> wait for the stdio, HTTP, and SSE servers in <code>options.mcpServers</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Three waits apply whatever value you set, including <code>0</code>:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>A <code>permissionPromptToolName</code> server</strong>: up to <code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code>.</li>
<li><strong>An in-process <a href="#sdk-mcp-servers" class="link">SDK server</a></strong>: up to <code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code> per connect attempt.</li>
<li><strong>A still-connecting <a href="#named-servers" class="link">named server</a></strong>: at least up to 2 seconds, so a value below <code>2000</code> doesn’t shorten its wait.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Set the variable to <code>0</code> to skip the wait for every other server. Servers still pending when the wait ends keep connecting in the background. <code>CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS</code> requires Claude Code v2.1.274 or later.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="make-startup-wait-for-servers" aria-labelledby="_R_1v9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#make-startup-wait-for-servers" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Make startup wait for servers"></a><span id="_R_1v9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Make startup wait for servers</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Two settings make startup itself wait, before Claude Code sends the init message:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_CONNECTION_NONBLOCKING</code></a> set to <code>0</code></strong>: startup waits for the whole connection batch, up to 5 seconds by default. Set <a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS</code></a>, in milliseconds, to change that cap. Servers still pending at the cap keep connecting in the background.</li>
<li><strong><code>alwaysLoad: true</code> on a server’s config</strong>: startup waits up to the same cap for that server’s tools, so the first turn has them at full schema, <a href="/docs/en/mcp#exempt-a-server-from-deferral" class="link">exempt from tool search deferral</a>. A remote server with a cached tool list supplies them without connecting or holding startup.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The init message reports each server’s status as of the moment Claude Code sends it.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="allow-mcp-tools" aria-labelledby="_R_279deehkd74t9_"><a href="#allow-mcp-tools" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Allow MCP tools"></a><span id="_R_279deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Allow MCP tools</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MCP tools require explicit permission before Claude can use them. Without permission, Claude will see that tools are available but won’t be able to call them.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="tool-naming-convention" aria-labelledby="_R_2b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#tool-naming-convention" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Tool naming convention"></a><span id="_R_2b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Tool naming convention</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP tools are named <code>mcp__&lt;server-name&gt;__&lt;tool-name&gt;</code>, where <code>&lt;server-name&gt;</code> is the name you register the server under, such as its key in <code>mcpServers</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Characters in the server name such as <code>.</code> and spaces are replaced by <code>_</code>. This table shows the full name of a <code>list_issues</code> tool for two server keys:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_2h9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">Key in <code>mcpServers</code></th><th style="text-align:left">Full tool name</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>github</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>mcp__github__list_issues</code></td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>my.server</code></td><td style="text-align:left"><code>mcp__my_server__list_issues</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Use the full tool names in <code>allowedTools</code> and in <a href="/docs/en/permissions#mcp" class="link">MCP permission rules</a>. For a server that a <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/plugins" class="link">plugin</a> provides, <code>&lt;server-name&gt;</code> takes the <a href="/docs/en/mcp#plugin-provided-mcp-servers" class="link">plugin-scoped form</a> and starts with <code>plugin_</code>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="auto-approve-with-allowedtools" aria-labelledby="_R_2l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#auto-approve-with-allowedtools" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Auto-approve with allowedTools"></a><span id="_R_2l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Auto-approve with allowedTools</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use <code>allowedTools</code> to auto-approve specific MCP tools:</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Wildcards (<code>*</code>) let you allow all tools from a server without listing each one individually.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutNote"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>Prefer <code>allowedTools</code> over permission modes for MCP access.</strong> <code>permissionMode: &quot;acceptEdits&quot;</code> does not auto-approve MCP tools (only file edits and filesystem Bash commands). <code>permissionMode: &quot;bypassPermissions&quot;</code> does auto-approve MCP tools but also disables most other safety prompts, which is broader than necessary; see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#how-permissions-are-evaluated" class="link">How permissions are evaluated</a> for the prompts that remain. A wildcard in <code>allowedTools</code> grants exactly the MCP server you want and nothing more. See <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#permission-modes" class="link">Permission modes</a> for a full comparison.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="discover-available-tools" aria-labelledby="_R_2v9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#discover-available-tools" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Discover available tools"></a><span id="_R_2v9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Discover available tools</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">To see what tools an MCP server provides, check the server’s documentation or inspect the <code>tools</code> array in the <code>system</code> init message. MCP tool names start with <code>mcp__</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code emits the init message after the <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">first-turn connection wait</a> for servers passed in <code>options.mcpServers</code>, so the <code>tools</code> array lists the <code>mcp__</code> tools of each server that has connected by then, plus those of servers with a <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">cached tool list</a>, which connect on first use. Tools of any other server that hasn’t connected are absent; see <a href="#error-handling" class="link">Error handling</a> for reading each server’s status.</span>
<span data-as="p">This filter prints the MCP tool names:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">You can also ask Claude to list the tools available from a server.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="transport-types" aria-labelledby="_R_3b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#transport-types" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Transport types"></a><span id="_R_3b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Transport types</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers communicate with your agent using different transport protocols. Check the server’s documentation to see which transport it supports:</span>
<ul>
<li>If the docs give you a <strong>command to run</strong> (like <code>npx @modelcontextprotocol/server-filesystem</code>), use stdio</li>
<li>If the docs give you a <strong>URL</strong>, use HTTP or SSE</li>
<li>If you’re building your own tools in code, use an SDK MCP server</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="stdio-servers" aria-labelledby="_R_3h9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#stdio-servers" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: stdio servers"></a><span id="_R_3h9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">stdio servers</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Local processes that communicate via stdin/stdout. Use this for MCP servers you run on the same machine. For the <code>.mcp.json</code> form, use the same fields shown at <a href="#from-a-config-file" class="link">Add a server from a config file</a>. In code, pass the command and its arguments. Replace <code>/Users/me/projects</code> with a directory on your machine:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">For the streamable HTTP transport, use <code>&quot;type&quot;: &quot;http&quot;</code> instead. In <code>.mcp.json</code> and other JSON config files, <code>&quot;streamable-http&quot;</code> is accepted as an alias for <code>&quot;http&quot;</code>. The SDKs’ <code>McpHttpServerConfig</code> type declares only <code>&quot;http&quot;</code>, so use <code>&quot;http&quot;</code> for servers you pass in code.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="sdk-mcp-servers" aria-labelledby="_R_3v9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#sdk-mcp-servers" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: SDK MCP servers"></a><span id="_R_3v9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">SDK MCP servers</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Define custom tools directly in your application code instead of running a separate server process. See the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/custom-tools" class="link">custom tools guide</a> for implementation details.</span>
<span data-as="p">An SDK MCP server registered by an <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#sdkcontrolinitializeresponse" class="link"><code>initialize</code> control request</a> begins connecting as soon as Claude Code processes the request.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="mcp-tool-search" aria-labelledby="_R_459deehkd74t9_"><a href="#mcp-tool-search" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: MCP tool search"></a><span id="_R_459deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">MCP tool search</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">When you have many MCP tools configured, tool definitions can consume a significant portion of your context window. Tool search solves this by withholding tool definitions from context and loading only the ones Claude needs for each turn.</span>
<span data-as="p">Tool search is enabled by default. See <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/tool-search" class="link">Tool search</a> for configuration options, best practices, and using tool search with custom SDK tools.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="authentication" aria-labelledby="_R_4b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#authentication" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Authentication"></a><span id="_R_4b9deehkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Authentication</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Most MCP servers require authentication to access external services. Pass credentials through environment variables in the server configuration.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="pass-credentials-via-environment-variables" aria-labelledby="_R_4f9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#pass-credentials-via-environment-variables" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Pass credentials via environment variables"></a><span id="_R_4f9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Pass credentials via environment variables</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use the <code>env</code> field to pass API keys, tokens, and other credentials to the MCP server:</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      &quot;headers&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        &quot;Authorization&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Bearer ${API_TOKEN}&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><span data-as="p">The <code>${API_TOKEN}</code> syntax expands environment variables at runtime.</span></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For a complete working example of a remote server authenticated with headers, see <a href="#list-issues-from-a-repository" class="link">List issues from a repository</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="oauth2-authentication" aria-labelledby="_R_4t9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#oauth2-authentication" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: OAuth2 authentication"></a><span id="_R_4t9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">OAuth2 authentication</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/specification/2025-03-26/basic/authorization" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">MCP specification supports OAuth 2.1</a> for authorization. The SDK doesn’t open a browser or run an interactive OAuth flow. When a configured server returns an authorization challenge and Claude Code has no token to send, the SDK reports the server as <code>needs-auth</code> or <code>failed</code>:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>needs-auth</code></strong>: Claude Code found no sign-in it can use for the server. Pass a token in <code>headers</code>, or <a href="/docs/en/mcp#authenticate-with-remote-mcp-servers" class="link">sign in from an interactive Claude Code session</a> on the same machine before the run.</li>
<li><strong><code>failed</code></strong>, with an <code>error</code> in its <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#mcpserverstatus" class="link"><code>McpServerStatus</code></a> entry that starts with <code>Couldn&#x27;t read the stored credentials</code> or <code>A request to MCP server</code>: Claude Code couldn’t read its credential store. For what to do, see <a href="/docs/en/errors#couldnt-read-the-stored-credentials-for-mcp-server" class="link">Couldn’t read the stored credentials for MCP server</a>. Before Claude Code v2.1.307, the status was <code>needs-auth</code> instead when a Mac’s login keychain was locked.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">In either case, the agent run continues without that server’s tools. The <code>mcp_servers</code> array of the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#sdksystemmessage" class="link">system init message</a> may still show <code>pending</code> for that server when it’s emitted. To confirm whether a server needs credentials, poll <code>mcpServerStatus()</code> in the TypeScript SDK or <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#methods" class="link"><code>get_mcp_status()</code></a> in Python.</span>
<span data-as="p">To supply credentials, complete the OAuth flow in your own application and pass the resulting access token in the server’s <code>headers</code>:</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="examples" aria-labelledby="_R_599deehkd74t9_"><a href="#examples" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Examples"></a><span id="_R_599deehkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Examples</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="list-issues-from-a-repository" aria-labelledby="_R_5b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#list-issues-from-a-repository" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: List issues from a repository"></a><span id="_R_5b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">List issues from a repository</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This example connects to the remote <a href="https://github.com/github/github-mcp-server" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">GitHub MCP server</a> to list recent issues. The example includes debug logging to verify the MCP connection and tool calls.</span>
<span data-as="p">Before running, create a <a href="https://github.com/settings/personal-access-tokens" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">GitHub personal access token</a> with read access to the repositories you want to query and set it as an environment variable:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">asyncio.run(main())</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">In the <code>MCP servers:</code> line, a <code>status</code> of <code>connected</code> for <code>github</code> confirms the token works. If Claude Code has a <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">cached tool list</a> for the server, the status can read <code>pending</code> instead and the server connects on its first tool call. If the status is <code>failed</code> or <code>needs-auth</code>, see <a href="#error-handling" class="link">Error handling</a> before trusting the result, since Claude can fall back to built-in tools when the server is unavailable.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="query-a-database" aria-labelledby="_R_5n9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#query-a-database" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Query a database"></a><span id="_R_5n9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Query a database</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This example uses <a href="https://github.com/bytebase/dbhub" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">DBHub</a> to query a Postgres database. The agent automatically discovers the database schema, writes the SQL query, and returns the results.</span>
<span data-as="p">DBHub’s <code>execute_sql</code> tool runs whatever SQL the agent emits, including writes, unless you restrict it. Setting <code>readonly = true</code> in the <a href="https://dbhub.ai/config/toml" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">DBHub configuration file</a> makes DBHub reject <code>INSERT</code>, <code>UPDATE</code>, <code>DELETE</code>, and DDL statements, so the example cannot modify your data even if the agent emits a write. DBHub resolves <code>${DATABASE_URL}</code> from the process environment when it loads the config, so the connection string stays out of the file. Create this <code>dbhub.toml</code> next to your script:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/5 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border <span data-as="p">The script then points DBHub at the config file instead of passing a connection string directly. Before running, set the <code>DATABASE_URL</code> environment variable to your connection string. Replace the placeholder values with your own database details:</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="error-handling" aria-labelledby="_R_659deehkd74t9_"><a href="#error-handling" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Error handling"></a><span id="_R_659deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Error handling</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MCP servers can fail to connect for various reasons: the server process might not be installed, credentials might be invalid, or a remote server might be unreachable.</span>
<span data-as="p">Claude Code emits a <code>system</code> message with subtype <code>init</code> at the start of each query. This message includes the connection status for each MCP server. The <code>status</code> field can be <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;connected&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;failed&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;needs-auth&quot;</code>, or <code>&quot;disabled&quot;</code>. Claude Code emits the init message after the <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">first-turn connection wait</a> for servers passed in <code>options.mcpServers</code>, so such a server that connected within the wait shows <code>&quot;connected&quot;</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">In the init message, don’t treat <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code> as a failure on its own. It can mean any of these:</span>
<ul>
<li>The server hasn’t connected yet. See <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">how long Claude Code waits for it before the first turn</a></li>
<li>The server’s tool list was <a href="#connection-timing" class="link">served from the cache</a>, with a connection made on first use</li>
<li>The connection deadline expired. Such a server reports <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code> or <code>&quot;failed&quot;</code> depending on timing</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Check for <code>&quot;failed&quot;</code> or <code>&quot;needs-auth&quot;</code> to detect servers that won’t be usable:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">        # failure was an error result, the error subtype branch above has</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">        # &quot;pending&quot; at init need a later status check.</span></span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">A remote server’s status can also change after it reports <code>&quot;connected&quot;</code>. Claude Code moves the server back to <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code> while <a href="/docs/en/mcp#automatic-reconnection" class="link">reconnecting</a>. A later <code>mcpServerStatus()</code> call in TypeScript, or <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#methods" class="link"><code>ClaudeSDKClient.get_mcp_status()</code></a> in Python, can then report <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code> for a server you saw connected earlier, with no configuration change on your side.</span>
<span data-as="p">After five reconnection attempts fail, the server reports <code>&quot;failed&quot;</code>, or <code>&quot;needs-auth&quot;</code> when it needs authorizing again. A <code>&quot;failed&quot;</code> HTTP or SSE server can still return to <code>&quot;connected&quot;</code> without a call from you, because Claude Code keeps <a href="/docs/en/mcp#mid-session-drops-of-a-remote-server" class="link">retrying in the background</a> after a transient error. To retry manually, call <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#methods" class="link"><code>reconnectMcpServer()</code></a> in TypeScript or <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#methods" class="link"><code>ClaudeSDKClient.reconnect_mcp_server()</code></a> in Python.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="troubleshooting" aria-labelledby="_R_6n9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#troubleshooting" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Troubleshooting"></a><span id="_R_6n9deehkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Troubleshooting</span></h2>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">            print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">f</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Server </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">{</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">server[</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&#x27;name&#x27;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> failed to connect&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">A <code>&quot;pending&quot;</code> status doesn’t mean the server failed. See <a href="#error-handling" class="link">Error handling</a> for the cases it covers at init. To get updated statuses later in the session, call the query’s <code>mcpServerStatus()</code> method in the TypeScript SDK, or <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#methods" class="link"><code>ClaudeSDKClient.get_mcp_status()</code></a> in Python.</span>
<span data-as="p">Common causes:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Missing environment variables</strong>: Ensure required tokens and credentials are set. For stdio servers, check the <code>env</code> field matches what the server expects.</li>
<li><strong>Server not installed</strong>: For <code>npx</code> commands, verify the package exists and Node.js is in your PATH.</li>
<li><strong>Invalid connection string</strong>: For database servers, verify the connection string format and that the database is accessible.</li>
<li><strong>Network issues</strong>: For remote HTTP/SSE servers, check the URL is reachable and any firewalls allow the connection.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="tools-not-being-called" aria-labelledby="_R_759deehkd74t9_"><a href="#tools-not-being-called" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Tools not being called"></a><span id="_R_759deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Tools not being called</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">If Claude sees tools but doesn’t use them, check that you’ve granted permission with <code>allowedTools</code>:</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="a-tool-is-missing-from-an-sdk-mcp-server" aria-labelledby="_R_7b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#a-tool-is-missing-from-an-sdk-mcp-server" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: A tool is missing from an SDK MCP server"></a><span id="_R_7b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">A tool is missing from an SDK MCP server</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">In the TypeScript SDK, when a tool’s input schema can’t be converted to JSON Schema, the server you created with <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#createsdkmcpserver" class="link"><code>createSdkMcpServer()</code></a> leaves that tool out when it lists its tools. The SDK emits a warning at that point. Under Node.js the warning is a process warning with code <code>CLAUDE_SDK_MCP_TOOL_SCHEMA_UNCONVERTIBLE</code>, and it starts with this text:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="text"><code language="text" numberOfLines="2"><span class="line"><span>Tool &quot;&lt;name&gt;&quot; on SDK MCP server &quot;&lt;server&gt;&quot; was left out of the server&#x27;s tool list, because its input schema cannot be converted to JSON Schema</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The rest of the warning gives the conversion error’s message when it has one, then says what to check and change.</span>
<span data-as="p">Before TypeScript Agent SDK v0.3.286, one unconvertible schema made the server’s whole tool listing fail without this warning, so none of that server’s tools reached Claude.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="connection-timeouts" aria-labelledby="_R_7l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#connection-timeouts" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Connection timeouts"></a><span id="_R_7l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Connection timeouts</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">MCP server connections time out after 30 seconds by default. To change how long a running tool call may take, set <a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT</code></a>. If your server takes longer to start, the connection fails. Raise the connection limit with the <a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MCP_TIMEOUT</code></a> environment variable, in milliseconds. For servers that need more startup time, also consider:</span>
<ul>
<li>Using a lighter-weight server if available</li>
<li>Pre-warming the server before starting your agent</li>
<li>Checking server logs for slow initialization causes</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">In TypeScript, you can set the tool-call limit for a single <a href="#sdk-mcp-servers" class="link">SDK MCP server</a> by passing <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#createsdkmcpserver" class="link"><code>timeout</code> to <code>createSdkMcpServer()</code></a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="tool-output-exceeds-maximum-allowed-tokens" aria-labelledby="_R_7t9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#tool-output-exceeds-maximum-allowed-tokens" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Tool output exceeds maximum allowed tokens"></a><span id="_R_7t9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Tool output exceeds maximum allowed tokens</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The SDK applies the same MCP output limits as Claude Code. When a successful tool result with no image content is larger than 25,000 tokens, Claude Code saves the output to a file and replaces the tool result with an error message that names the file path, so the agent can read the output back in portions.</span>
<span data-as="p">To change the token limit, set the <a href="/docs/en/env-vars" class="link"><code>MAX_MCP_OUTPUT_TOKENS</code></a> environment variable. Unless the tool declares <code>anthropic/maxResultSizeChars</code>, a successful text result longer than 50,000 characters is saved to a file regardless of the token limit. See <a href="/docs/en/mcp#mcp-output-limits-and-warnings" class="link">MCP output limits and warnings</a> for the full behavior, including how a server declares that annotation.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="related-resources" aria-labelledby="_R_839deehkd74t9_"><a href="#related-resources" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Related resources"></a><span id="_R_839deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Related resources</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/custom-tools" class="link">Custom tools guide</a></strong>: Build your own MCP server that runs in-process with your SDK application</li>
<li><strong><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions" class="link">Permissions</a></strong>: Control which MCP tools your agent can use with <code>allowedTools</code> and <code>disallowedTools</code></li>
<li><strong><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript" class="link">TypeScript SDK reference</a></strong>: Full API reference including MCP configuration options</li>
<li><strong><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python" class="link">Python SDK reference</a></strong>: Full API reference including MCP configuration options</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/servers" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">MCP server directory</a></strong>: Browse available MCP servers for databases, APIs, and more</li>

timing`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Claude Code starts connecting your MCP servers when the session starts, and sends the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#error-handling`,children:`init message`}),` once the first-turn wait, if any, ends. How long the first turn waits for a server depends on where you configured it. If you set `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`permissionPromptToolName`}),`, the first turn always waits for that tool’s server, up to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`}),`, wherever you configured it.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`For a server in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`options.mcpServers`}),`, find its type in the first column to see how long the first turn waits for it.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Table,{children:[_jsx(_components.thead,{children:_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Server type`}),_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Delays the first turn?`}),_jsx(_components.th,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`First-turn wait timeout`})]})}),_jsxs(_components.tbody,{children:[_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`stdio server, or HTTP/SSE server without a cached tool list`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Yes, until it connects`}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/env-vars`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`})}),`, 30 seconds by default; the connection fails at that deadline`]})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Remote server with a cached tool list, saved by Claude Code from a previous connection`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`No; the cached tools are available from the first turn`}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`None; connects on its first tool call, and that deferred connect has its own timeout`})]}),_jsxs(_components.tr,{children:[_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[`In-process `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#sdk-mcp-servers`,children:`SDK server`})]}),_jsx(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:`Yes, until it connects and lists its tools`}),_jsxs(_components.td,{style:{textAlign:`left`},children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/env-vars`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`})}),`, 30 seconds by default, per connect attempt; the connection fails at that deadline`]})]})]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`A server has a cached tool list only when the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#server-status-detail`,children:`MCP discovery cache`}),` is on.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Servers from `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#from-a-config-file`,children:`settings files`}),` such as `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`.mcp.json`}),`, or from plugins, often still show `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`pending`}),` in the init message. How long the first turn waits for them depends on `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`options.mcpServers`}),` and tool search:`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`options.mcpServers`}),` includes a stdio, HTTP, or SSE server`]}),`: the first turn waits for the pending settings-file and plugin servers too, up to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[`Otherwise, when `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`}),` points to a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#tool-search-through-a-gateway`,children:`gateway`}),` and `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#configure-tool-search`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH`})}),` is unset`]}),`: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds for every pending settings-file and plugin server.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[`Otherwise, with `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/tool-search`,children:`tool search`}),` on, which is the default`]}),`: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds, and only for pending servers that set `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#exempt-a-server-from-deferral`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`alwaysLoad: true`})}),` or that you `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#named-servers`,children:`named`}),`. The rest keep connecting in the background, and Claude `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#tool-availability`,children:`reaches their tools`}),` once they connect.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Otherwise, with tool search off`}),`: the first turn waits up to 2 seconds for every pending settings-file and plugin server. Tool search is off when your `,_jsxs(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/tool-search#configure-tool-search`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH`}),` value or model`]}),` turns it off, or when you exclude the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ToolSearch`}),` tool, for example through `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`disallowedTools`}),`.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`named-servers`,children:`Named servers`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`You name a server by listing any of its tools, such as `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`mcp__github__list_issues`}),` or `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`mcp__github__*`}),`, in `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#allow-mcp-tools`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`allowedTools`})}),` or in a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`permissions.allow`}),` rule in the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`settings`}),` option. An `,_jsxs(_components.a,{href:`/en/hooks#mcp-tool-hook-fields`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`mcp_tool`}),` hook`]}),` also names the server it calls when you configure it on a prompt-submission, tool-use, permission, subagent, task, elicitation, or worktree event. Before v2.1.284, naming a server had no effect on the first-turn wait.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`set-the-first-turn-wait-yourself`,children:`Set the first-turn wait yourself`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`To choose the first-turn wait yourself, set `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS`}),` in the `,_jsxs(_components.a,{href:`/en/agent-sdk/configuration#set-environment-variables`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`env`}),` option`]}),`, for example `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS: \"5000\"`}),`. The first turn then waits up to that many milliseconds for pending settings-file and plugin servers, whether or not tool search is on. The same limit replaces the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`}),` wait for the stdio, HTTP, and SSE servers in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`options.mcpServers`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Three waits apply whatever value you set, including `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`0`}),`:`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[`A `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`permissionPromptToolName`}),` server`]}),`: up to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[`An in-process `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#sdk-mcp-servers`,children:`SDK server`})]}),`: up to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_TIMEOUT`}),` per connect attempt.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[`A still-connecting `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`#named-servers`,children:`named server`})]}),`: at least up to 2 seconds, so a value below `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`2000`}),` doesn’t shorten its wait.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Set the variable to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`0`}),` to skip the wait for every other server. Servers still pending when the wait ends keep connecting in the background. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_STARTUP_WAIT_MS`}),` requires Claude Code v2.1.274 or later.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`make-startup-wait-for-servers`,children:`Make startup wait for servers`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Two settings make startup itself wait, before Claude Code sends the init message:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/env-vars`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_CONNECTION_NONBLOCKING`})}),` set to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`0`})]}),`: startup waits for the whole connection batch, up to 5 seconds by default. Set `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/env-vars`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCP_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`})}),`, in milliseconds, to change that cap. Servers still pending at the cap keep connecting in the background.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`alwaysLoad: true`}),` on a server’s config`]}),`: startup waits up to the same cap for that server’s tools, so the first turn has them at full schema, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/en/mcp#exempt-a-server-from-deferral`,children:`exempt from tool search deferral`}),`. A remote server with a cached tool list supplies them without connecting or holding startup.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`The init message reports each server’s status as of the moment Claude Code sends it.`}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`allow-mcp-tools`,children:`Allow MCP tools`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`MCP tools require explicit permission before Claude can use them.
